cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.20)

# no flags of CMake's own: every flag comes from the resolved state

# Make's link shape: objects first, then the link flags
set(CMAKE_C_LINK_EXECUTABLE "<CMAKE_C_COMPILER> <OBJECTS> <LINK_FLAGS> -o <TARGET>")

set_target_properties(gnrc_networking.elf PROPERTIES LINK_FLAGS "-lm -L/riot/cpu/nrf52/ldscripts -L/riot/cpu/cortexm_common/ldscripts -Tcortexm.ld -Wl,--fatal-warnings -mcpu=cortex-m4 -mlittle-endian -mthumb -mfloat-abi=hard -mfpu=fpv4-sp-d16 -ggdb -g3 -Os -static -lgcc -nostartfiles -Wl,--gc-sections -Wl,--defsym=_rom_start_addr=0x00000000 -Wl,--defsym=_ram_start_addr=0x20000000 -Wl,--defsym=_rom_length=0x100000 -Wl,--defsym=_ram_length=0x40000 -L/riot/core/ldscripts -z noexecstack -Wl,-wrap=malloc -Wl,-wrap=calloc -Wl,-wrap=realloc -Wl,-wrap=free -specs=nano.specs -lc -Wl,-wrap=__assert_func -Wl,--defsym=_slot_aux_offset=1048576 -Wl,--defsym=_slot_aux_len=0 -Wl,-wrap=printf -Wl,-wrap=fprintf -Wl,-wrap=dprintf -Wl,-wrap=sprintf -Wl,-wrap=snprintf -Wl,-wrap=vprintf -Wl,-wrap=vfprintf -Wl,-wrap=vdprintf -Wl,-wrap=vsprintf -Wl,-wrap=vsnprintf -Wl,-wrap=putchar -Wl,-wrap=putc -Wl,-wrap=fputc -Wl,-wrap=puts -Wl,-wrap=fputs -Wl,-wrap=_printf_common -Wl,-wrap=_fprintf_r -Wl,-wrap=_printf_r -Wl,-wrap=iprintf -Wl,-wrap=fiprintf -Wl,-Map=/riot/examples/networking/gnrc/networking/bin/nrf52840dk/gnrc_networking.map")
# group 2A: resolved state, printed without building
add_custom_target(info-modules COMMAND cat ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/modules.txt VERBATIM)
add_custom_target(info-cpu COMMAND echo "nrf52" VERBATIM)
# group 2B: existing ELF / programmer / terminal, no build
add_custom_target(info-buildsize COMMAND "arm-none-eabi-size" "-d" "-B" $<TARGET_FILE:gnrc_networking.elf> VERBATIM)
add_custom_target(flash-only COMMAND "/riot/dist/tools/openocd/openocd.sh" "flash" $<TARGET_FILE:gnrc_networking.elf> USES_TERMINAL VERBATIM)
add_custom_target(term COMMAND "/riot/dist/tools/pyterm/pyterm" "-p" "/dev/ttyACM0" "-b" "115200" "-ln" "/tmp/pyterm-" "-rn" "2026-11-14_16.33.09-gnrc_networking-nrf52840dk" USES_TERMINAL VERBATIM)
# group 3: build, then flash
add_custom_target(flash COMMAND "/riot/dist/tools/openocd/openocd.sh" "flash" $<TARGET_FILE:gnrc_networking.elf> DEPENDS gnrc_networking.elf USES_TERMINAL VERBATIM)
